# CNC Twin validation report

Generated by `twin validate` (`cnctwin.validation`, see docs/validation.md) from the cases in `validation/cases/` against the budgets of `validation/budgets.yaml`. Every case is simulated by its quantity's harness with the domain libraries at the level the experiment measured; models take every parameter from the databases and the case's stated inputs (nothing is tuned to a reference). Errors are relative to the reference (temperatures: to the rise above ambient). Cases whose reference also built a database value the model used are **calibration-dependent** and are reported separately; verdicts use the independent cases only.

* **Pass**: none.
* **Fail**: chip_ratio, chip_temperature, shear_angle, tool_temperature.
* Not judged, no budget in validation/budgets.yaml: chip_form, coolant_reach, cycle_time, spindle_growth, taylor_life.
* No harness yet: cutting_force, drilling_thrust, drilling_torque, roughness, stability_lobe, wall_error.

Budget rule (validation/budgets.yaml, applied as written): tolerance = max(budget_pct/100 x |reference| (for temperatures: x the rise above ambient), 2 x the reference's uncertainty); a case passes when every compared value lies within its tolerance; a quantity passes when at least 80 % of its cases pass and no case misses by more than twice its tolerance. Values without a prediction count as misses; values marked 'not compared' are listed with the reason but do not count. The error statistics are over the compared scalar values of the independent cases (reference values and curve points; temperatures as rises above ambient); classifications are counted as matches.

Calibration-dependent cases (excluded from the verdicts):

* `chipform-tungaloy-cnmg120408-tm-steel` (chip_form): tool tungaloy-cnmg120408-tm-t9215-pclnr2525m12e chip breaker window from `tungaloy-2023-gc-insert`
* `chipratio-orth-ti64-ducobu2017` (chip_ratio): ti6al4v flow stress (Johnson-Cook) from `ducobu-2017-jc-ti6al4v`
* `chipratio-orth-ti64-klippel2022-vc70` (chip_ratio): ti6al4v friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) from `klippel-2024-ck45-orthogonal`
* `taylor-aouici2010-cbn7020-h11-50hrc-dry` (taylor_life): h13-50hrc tool_life[0] (cbn, uncoated) from `aouici-2010-h11-cbn`
* `taylor-bouchelaghem2007-cbn-d3-60hrc-dry` (taylor_life): d2-60hrc tool_life[0] (cbn, TiN) from `bouchelaghem-2007-d3-cbn`

## chip_form

* Budget: none in validation/budgets.yaml.
* Verdict: not judged (no budget) - validation/budgets.yaml defines no budget for this quantity.
* Harness: `cnctwin.validation.harnesses.chipform.chip_form`.
* Oxley chip state and cnctwin.mechanics.chipform (curl, breaker window, Nakayama breaking, ISO 3685 class, piece length) per tested feed and depth; the length is mapped to the case's own classes, or a feed-depth grid gives the chip-control window.

| group | cases | cases within budget | counted values (no prediction) | median abs. error (all / values / curve points) | p90 abs. error | class matches | values within uncertainty | largest miss (tolerances) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| independent | 2 | - | 32 (0) | - / - / - | - | 6/32 | - | - |
| calibration-dependent | 1 | - | 9 (0) | 75.4 % / 74.7 % / 75.4 % | 91.8 % | - | 0/9 | - |

**Failures and likely causes.** No budget is defined for chip_form. The chip-form model's breaking criterion and curl radii are estimates (mechanics.md 13) and the case inserts' breaker geometries (land, groove width and depth) are mostly not in the database, so the comparison tests the defaults.

### chipform-slodki2019-cnmg120404-sf-ti64eli-hpc

* Source: `slodki-2019-ti64-eli-hpc-turning`, Sect. 2 (material, insert, holder, coolant), Tables 2-3 (plan with vc per trial), Sect. 3 (grading rule), Table 9 (grade and cut cross-section per feed and depth); Table 8 holds the chip photographs (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/chip_form/chipform-slodki2019-cnmg120404-sf-ti64eli-hpc.yaml`; operation longitudinal_turning; material `ti6al4v`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.oxley.solve -> cnctwin.mechanics.chipform.chip_form.
* Settings: breaking_codes = [22, 23, 31, 32, 42, 43, 51, 52, 61, 62, 70], coating = none, db_insert = -, edge_radius_um = 35, entering_angle_deg = 95, rake_deg = -6, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.coolant.delivery` = through the PCLNR2020K12HP high-pressure holder onto the rake face (the paper names the HP holder and the pressure but not the nozzle size, flow or jet angle).
* Inputs the harness assumed: ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); tool material = carbide-k20-6co (db/tool_materials/carbide-k20-6co.yaml names Sandvik H13A (straight WC-6Co), db/tool_materials); chip breaker geometry = none (natural curl) (the SF breaker's land and groove are not in the database, db/tools); rake and inclination = -6 / -6 deg (not stated: a negative ISO holder for a negative insert, est); entering angle = 95 deg (not stated: the C-insert holders named (PCLNR, PCMNN) are 95 deg holders, est); cutting-edge radius = 35 um (not stated in the case: the tooling model's default for this tool (insert_edge_radius_m), db/models/tooling.yaml); coolant emulsion-8pct = friction factor 0.83 (the case's emulsion is not in the database: the database emulsion, full reach of the jet at the rake face, db/coolants).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); ti6al4v flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`ducobu-2017-jc-ti6al4v`); ti6al4v physical.density_kg_m3 (`aubert-duval-2018-ta6v`); ti6al4v physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v physical.conductivity_W_mK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`wojciechowski-2022-min-chip-thickness`); ti6al4v friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`klippel-2024-ck45-orthogonal`).
* Material: database entry ti6al4v; the case says: Grade 5 ELI, hot-forged, annealed and peeled bar D 50 mm; Rm 902 MPa, 29 HRC; Al 6.10, V 4.13, Fe 0.05, O 0.10 % (Table 1)
* High-pressure coolant enters as a friction factor; the jet's chip-breaking action is not modelled.
* Result: 16 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; 5/16 classes match; case not judged (no budget).
* Likely cause: 11 of 16 compared values miss. Without the breaker's groove geometry the model curls the chip with its natural radius (15 x contact length, est) and breaks it only when the bending strain t2/2R exceeds 0.1 x the bulk fracture strain (est): it predicts unbroken chips everywhere; the HPC jet's breaking action is not modelled.

### chipform-tungaloy-cnmg120408-tm-steel

* Source: `tungaloy-2023-gc-turning-us`, p. B057 (PDF p. 65), 'Chip control range' chart for P, TM outline (digitised); p. B004 (PDF p. 12), standard cutting conditions for TM (measured, digitised, stated uncertainty 2 %).
* File: `validation/cases/chip_form/chipform-tungaloy-cnmg120408-tm-steel.yaml`; operation longitudinal_turning; material `aisi1045`.
* Validation class: **calibration-dependent**: tool tungaloy-cnmg120408-tm-t9215-pclnr2525m12e chip breaker window from `tungaloy-2023-gc-insert` (source).
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.oxley.solve -> cnctwin.mechanics.chipform.chip_form.
* Settings: breaking_codes = [22, 23, 31, 32, 42, 43, 51, 52, 61, 62, 70], coating = al2o3-cvd, db_insert = tungaloy-cnmg120408-tm-t9215-pclnr2525m12e, edge_radius_um = 35, entering_angle_deg = 95, rake_deg = 6, tool_material = carbide-p30.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.conditions.coolant` = emulsion (not stated); `setup.conditions.cutting_speed_m_min` = 250 (the chart states no speed; middle of the B004 range for TM / T9215 in medium carbon steels).
* Inputs the harness assumed: ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); entering angle = 95 deg (not stated: the C-insert holders named (PCLNR, PCMNN) are 95 deg holders, est); cutting-edge radius = 35 um (not stated in the case: the tooling model's default for this tool (insert_edge_radius_m), db/models/tooling.yaml).
* Database values used: tool tungaloy-cnmg120408-tm-t9215-pclnr2525m12e chip breaker window (`tungaloy-2023-gc-insert`); aisi1045 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`burns-2012-1045-cutting-temperature`); aisi1045 physical.density_kg_m3 (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`est`); aisi1045 friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`est`).
* Material: database entry aisi1045; the case says: the chart is for ISO group P without a named steel; B004 lists 1045 and 4140 as 'medium carbon steels, alloy steels' for the TM conditions
* Insert from the database: tungaloy-cnmg120408-tm-t9215-pclnr2525m12e (chip breaker TM, window and land/rake of the maker's catalogue).
* Result: 9 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 75.4 %, max 138.6 %; 0/9 within uncertainty; case not judged (no budget).
* Likely cause: 9 of 9 compared values miss. Without the breaker's groove geometry the model curls the chip with its natural radius (15 x contact length, est) and breaks it only when the bending strain t2/2R exceeds 0.1 x the bulk fracture strain (est): it predicts unbroken chips everywhere; the HPC jet's breaking action is not modelled. The case counts snarled tubular and washer chips (2.3, 4.3) and long conical helices (5.1) as controlled; the model's unbroken chips fall in these classes at every grid point, so the predicted window is the whole grid.

## chip_ratio

* Budget: 20 % (relative error of the simulated chip compression ratio h / h_chip (mean chip thickness for segmented chips)).
* Verdict: **fail** - only 0 of 3 cases within budget (0% < 80%); chipratio-orth-ck45-klippel2022-vc150 misses by inf tolerances (> 2).
* Harness: `cnctwin.validation.harnesses.orthogonal.chip_ratio`.
* Oxley's orthogonal theory (cnctwin.mechanics.oxley.solve) per test at t1 = h - h_min, tool and work at ambient; simulated ratio h / t2 against the measured h / h_chip.

| group | cases | cases within budget | counted values (no prediction) | median abs. error (all / values / curve points) | p90 abs. error | class matches | values within uncertainty | largest miss (tolerances) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| independent | 3 | 0/3 | 54 (2) | 19.6 % / 19.6 % / - | 77.4 % | - | 2/5 | inf (chipratio-orth-ck45-klippel2022-vc150) |
| calibration-dependent | 2 | 0/2 | 10 (0) | 20.9 % / 20.9 % / - | 49.0 % | - | 3/10 | 3.88 (chipratio-orth-ti64-klippel2022-vc70) |

**Failures and likely causes.** 0 of 3 independent cases within budget. Values within tolerance per case: chipratio-orth-ck45-klippel2022-f010 4/15; chipratio-orth-ck45-klippel2022-vc150 8/15; chipratio-orth-ss304-marcicano2005 15/24. Over the independent cases the simulated chip ratio is on average +28 % off (positive: the simulated chip is thinner than measured). With the Coulomb-Tresca interface in the sliding regime, Oxley's shear angle is set mainly by the friction angle (lambda = atan mu) and the strain-hardening term, so the simulated ratio barely changes with speed and feed while the measured one varies. The friction coefficients of the material files are estimates for Ck45 and 304; BUE (Ck45 below 70 m/min) and the ploughed layer h_min (an estimated ratio x the edge radius) decide the low-speed and small-h tests, where the model forms no chip at h = 10 um although the experiment did. The two Ti6Al4V cases are calibration-dependent (Johnson-Cook set chosen against the Ducobu experiments; friction from the ETH tests) and are reported apart. No coefficient was changed for this report.

### chipratio-orth-ck45-klippel2022-f010

* Source: `klippel-2022-arxiv-orthogonal-data`, Table B.15 (Ck45), column havg, tests V0072, V0073, V0074, V0125, V0162, V0084, V0085, V0093, V0094, V0095, V0236, V0245, V0293, V0299, V0300 (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/chip_ratio/chipratio-orth-ck45-klippel2022-f010.yaml`; operation orthogonal_turning; material `aisi1045`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.materials.WorkMaterial/ToolSide (databases) -> cnctwin.mechanics.contact.min_chip_thickness -> cnctwin.mechanics.oxley.solve (Oxley 1989 with Johnson-Cook, Coulomb-Tresca interface, rake heat partition at the tool temperature).
* Settings: coating = none, h_min_ratio = 0.27, mu_sliding = 0.5, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, tool_temperature = ambient (tool body at the start of a short test), work_temperature_K = 293.1.
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (db/tool_materials/carbide-k20-6co.yaml names Sandvik H13A (straight WC-6Co), db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); aisi1045 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`burns-2012-1045-cutting-temperature`); aisi1045 physical.density_kg_m3 (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`est`); aisi1045 friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`est`).
* Material: database entry aisi1045; the case says: Ck45 (C45E) rolled bar, one batch for all tests: supplier tensile test Rm 671 MPa, Rp0.2 420 MPa, A5 22.2 % (Table 2), ferritic-pearlitic; the outer scale and decarburised zone were turned off before the tests (Sect. 2.6). The DB entry is normalised AISI 1045 (+N, ~190 HB): use the batch strength above when calibrating flow stress.
* Result: 15 compared, 0 without prediction, 15 not compared; median abs. error 30.1 %, max 100.1 %; 0/2 within uncertainty; case **outside budget**; largest miss 5.01 tolerances.
* Likely cause: simulated ratio 0.468-0.544 against measured 0.241-0.679 (mean signed error +35 %); database sliding friction mu = 0.5, h_min ratio 0.27. Ck45 has a built-up edge at 10-50 m/min (database BUE window) that thickens the chip; the orthogonal solution has no BUE. The ETH data give an Albrecht friction coefficient of 0.78; the database keeps the estimate 0.5. With the Coulomb-Tresca interface in the sliding regime, Oxley's shear angle is set mainly by the friction angle (lambda = atan mu) and the strain-hardening term, so the simulated ratio barely changes with speed and feed while the measured one varies.

### chipratio-orth-ss304-marcicano2005

* Source: `marcicano-2005-aisi304-cutting-tests`, Tables 3 and 4, columns t0 and t1 (measured).

* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.clearance_deg` = 6 (assumed: not stated: ISO 1832 letter P = 11 deg insert clearance (code convention, standard not read) minus the 5 deg positive tilt that gives the stated rake); `setup.tool.edge_radius_um` = 15 (assumed: not stated; typical of a ground (G-tolerance) uncoated insert edge (est.)); `setup.tool.nose_radius_mm` = 0.8 (assumed: ISO code '08' = 0.8 mm corner radius (code convention); outside the cut when the tube wall lies on the straight edge); `setup.conditions.coolant` = dry (assumed: not stated; dry orthogonal tests are implied by the analysis).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (grade not in the database: the carbide class default, db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est).

* Result: 24 compared, 0 without prediction, 24 not compared; median abs. error 17.3 %, max 96.0 %; case **outside budget**; largest miss 4.8 tolerances.
* Likely cause: simulated ratio 0.432-0.459 against measured 0.234-0.541 (mean signed error +19 %); database sliding friction mu = 0.6, h_min ratio 0.27. With the Coulomb-Tresca interface in the sliding regime, Oxley's shear angle is set mainly by the friction angle (lambda = atan mu) and the strain-hardening term, so the simulated ratio barely changes with speed and feed while the measured one varies.

### chipratio-orth-ti64-ducobu2017

* Source: `ducobu-2017-ti6al4v-orthogonal-reference`, Table 3 (experimental reference: average RMS forces per mm width and average chip thickness for h = 40, 60, 100 um) (measured, stated uncertainty 10 %).
* File: `validation/cases/chip_ratio/chipratio-orth-ti64-ducobu2017.yaml`; operation orthogonal_turning; material `ti6al4v`.
* Validation class: **calibration-dependent**: ti6al4v flow stress (Johnson-Cook) from `ducobu-2017-jc-ti6al4v` (source).
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.materials.WorkMaterial/ToolSide (databases) -> cnctwin.mechanics.contact.min_chip_thickness -> cnctwin.mechanics.oxley.solve (Oxley 1989 with Johnson-Cook, Coulomb-Tresca interface, rake heat partition at the tool temperature).
* Settings: coating = none, h_min_ratio = 0.37, mu_sliding = 0.3, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, tool_temperature = ambient (tool body at the start of a short test), work_temperature_K = 293.1.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.coating` = none (assumed: not stated; the FE model of the same paper uses uncoated tungsten carbide); `setup.conditions.coolant` = dry (assumed: not stated in this paper).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (uncoated straight WC-Co: the straight WC-6Co K20 entry, db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); ti6al4v flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`ducobu-2017-jc-ti6al4v`); ti6al4v physical.density_kg_m3 (`aubert-duval-2018-ta6v`); ti6al4v physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v physical.conductivity_W_mK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`wojciechowski-2022-min-chip-thickness`); ti6al4v friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`klippel-2024-ck45-orthogonal`).
* Material: database entry ti6al4v; the case says: Ti6Al4V; heat treatment, hardness and supplier data are not given in the source (DB entry: mill annealed); the paper's model uses E 113.8 GPa, rho 4430 kg/m3, k 7.3 W/mK, cp 580 J/kgK (Table 4)
* Result: 3 compared, 0 without prediction, 3 not compared; median abs. error 7.7 %, max 28.1 %; 1/3 within uncertainty; case **outside budget**; largest miss 1.41 tolerances.
* Likely cause: simulated ratio 0.765-0.869 against measured 0.678-0.75 (mean signed error +13 %); database sliding friction mu = 0.3, h_min ratio 0.37. Ti6Al4V forms segmented chips (database tendency 'segmented'); Oxley's continuous-chip solution over-predicts the thickness ratio of segmented chips (mechanics.md 13). With the Coulomb-Tresca interface in the sliding regime, Oxley's shear angle is set mainly by the friction angle (lambda = atan mu) and the strain-hardening term, so the simulated ratio barely changes with speed and feed while the measured one varies.

| chip_ratio h40 (derived) | 40 um | 0.678 | 0.8687 |  | +28.1 % | 0.136 | **no** | h 0.040 mm, vc 30 m/min, r_e 20 um |
| h_chip h60 | 60 um | 80 | 74.3 | um | - | 16 | not compared | chip thickness: listed, the budget applies to the derived ratio |
| chip_ratio h60 (derived) | 60 um | 0.75 | 0.8076 |  | +7.7 % | 0.15 | yes | h 0.060 mm, vc 30 m/min, r_e 20 um |

## chip_temperature

* Budget: 20 % (relative error of the simulated chip (free-surface) temperature rise above ambient).
* Verdict: **fail** - only 0 of 1 cases within budget (0% < 80%); chiptemp-orth-ck45-klippel2022-temper misses by 3.48 tolerances (> 2).
* Harness: `cnctwin.validation.harnesses.temperature.chip_temperature`.
* Oxley's mean chip temperature per test (t1 = h - h_min, tool at ambient), its oxide exposure during the contact and the air cooling afterwards (cnctwin.mechanics.colour), read as the equivalent temperature of the material's tempering-colour table and capped at its top row, as the experimenters' reading was.

| group | cases | cases within budget | counted values (no prediction) | median abs. error (all / values / curve points) | p90 abs. error | class matches | values within uncertainty | largest miss (tolerances) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| independent | 1 | 0/1 | 24 (0) | 17.4 % / 17.4 % / - | 63.8 % | - | - | 3.48 (chiptemp-orth-ck45-klippel2022-temper) |

**Failures and likely causes.** 0 of 1 independent cases within budget. Values within tolerance per case: chiptemp-orth-ck45-klippel2022-temper 12/24. One indirect reference (tempering colours, capped at 360 C). The simulated reading is the colour-equivalent temperature of the chip's thermal history, so the oxide-growth estimates of mechanics-colour.yaml enter directly.

### chiptemp-orth-ck45-klippel2022-temper

* Source: `klippel-2022-arxiv-orthogonal-data`, Table B.15 (Ck45), column Tchip, tests V0072, V0073, V0074, V0123, V0124, V0125, V0160, V0161, V0162, V0084, V0085, V0093, V0094, V0095, V0236, V0245, V0246, V0247, V0292, V0293, V0294, V0298, V0299, V0300; Sect. 3.5 and Fig. 31 (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/chip_temperature/chiptemp-orth-ck45-klippel2022-temper.yaml`; operation orthogonal_turning; material `aisi1045`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.oxley.solve -> cnctwin.mechanics.colour.emitted_chip_colour -> cnctwin.mechanics.colour.equivalent_temperature -> cnctwin.thermal.convection.
* Settings: coating = none, colour_table_top_degC = 360, mu_sliding = 0.5, oxide_activation_K = 1.684e+04, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co.
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (db/tool_materials/carbide-k20-6co.yaml names Sandvik H13A (straight WC-6Co), db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); chip cooling after the contact = free convection + radiation in still air (cnctwin.thermal.convection: horizontal cylinder of the chip width, emissivity 0.35 (steel), est).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); aisi1045 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`burns-2012-1045-cutting-temperature`); aisi1045 physical.density_kg_m3 (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`est`); aisi1045 friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`est`); aisi1045 chip.oxide_colours (`wikipedia-de-anlassen`).
* Material: database entry aisi1045; the case says: Ck45 (C45E) rolled bar, one batch for all tests: supplier tensile test Rm 671 MPa, Rp0.2 420 MPa, A5 22.2 % (Table 2), ferritic-pearlitic; the outer scale and decarburised zone were turned off before the tests (Sect. 2.6). The DB entry is normalised AISI 1045 (+N, ~190 HB): use the batch strength above when calibrating flow stress.
* Result: 24 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 17.4 %, max 69.7 %; case **outside budget**; largest miss 3.48 tolerances.
* Likely cause: mean signed error -27 % of the rise. The reading depends on the oxide model (activation energy est., colour table read as 30-min holds) as much as on the chip temperature; at the colour table's top the reference is only a lower bound.

## coolant_reach

* Budget: none in validation/budgets.yaml.
* Verdict: not judged (no budget) - validation/budgets.yaml defines no budget for this quantity.
* Harness: `cnctwin.validation.harnesses.coolant.coolant_reach`.
* The coolant's effect on the measured quantity through cnctwin.coolant (reach of the jet at the cut, lubrication friction factor) applied to the mechanics: the contact temperature of a milling engagement (tool-work thermocouple), or the chip form and the cutting force of a turning cut.

| group | cases | cases within budget | counted values (no prediction) | median abs. error (all / values / curve points) | p90 abs. error | class matches | values within uncertainty | largest miss (tolerances) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| independent | 2 | - | 63 (0) | 38.6 % / 38.6 % / - | 85.4 % | 10/18 | - | - |

**Failures and likely causes.** No budget is defined for coolant_reach. In these set-ups the coolant model reaches the mechanics only through the lubrication friction factor, so measured effects of jet direction, convective cooling during the engagement and jet-assisted chip breaking are not reproduced.

### coolant-struzikiewicz2023-ti64-jet-direction

* Source: `struzikiewicz-2023-hpc-jet-direction`, Sect. 2 (setup), Fig. 1b (nozzle geometry: 0.7 mm bore, 20 mm distance, alpha/beta definition), Table 1 (material), Tables 3 and 5 (plan, Fc and Cch per test), Table 8 (chip-form grade) (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/coolant_reach/coolant-struzikiewicz2023-ti64-jet-direction.yaml`; operation longitudinal_turning; material `ti6al4v`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.coolant.lubrication.friction -> cnctwin.mechanics.chipform.chip_form -> cnctwin.mechanics.coefficients (cutting force).
* Settings: edge_radius_um = 35, fluid = emulsion-8pct, friction_factor = 0.8268, h_min_um = 12.95, rake_deg = -6.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.coolant.flow_l_min` = 2.7 (not stated; orifice estimate Q = Cd A sqrt(2 p / rho) with Cd 0.7, A of a 0.7 mm bore, p 150 bar, rho 1000 kg/m3).
* Inputs the harness assumed: ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); tool material = carbide-submicron-10co (Sandvik 1115: PVD-coated fine-grained S-grade substrate, db/tool_materials); coating = none (case coating 'PVD-coated grade (Sandvik 1115)' has no database entry: modelled uncoated, db/coatings); chip breaker geometry = none (natural curl) (the SGF breaker's land and groove are not in the database, db/tools); rake and inclination = -6 / -6 deg (not stated: a negative ISO holder for a negative insert, est); entering angle = 95 deg (not stated: the C-insert holders named (PCLNR, PCMNN) are 95 deg holders, est); cutting-edge radius = 35 um (not stated in the case: the tooling model's default for this tool (insert_edge_radius_m), db/models/tooling.yaml); coolant emulsion-8pct = friction factor 0.83 (the case's emulsion is not in the database: the database emulsion, full reach of the jet at the rake face, db/coolants).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-submicron-10co thermal properties (`hyperion-insert-blanks-grades`); ti6al4v flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`ducobu-2017-jc-ti6al4v`); ti6al4v physical.density_kg_m3 (`aubert-duval-2018-ta6v`); ti6al4v physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v physical.conductivity_W_mK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`wojciechowski-2022-min-chip-thickness`); ti6al4v friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`klippel-2024-ck45-orthogonal`).
* Material: database entry ti6al4v; the case says: Grade 5 ELI (extra-low interstitials) bar D 50 mm: Rm 902 MPa, Rp0.2 815 MPa, A 13 %, Z 49 %, 29 HRC (Table 1)
* Coolant 'Blaser Blasocut 2000 universal, 10 % emulsion' as emulsion-8pct; reach 1 for every jet direction (one free jet 20 mm from the corner aimed at the rake face).
* Result: 36 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 38.8 %, max 57.9 %; 10/18 classes match; case not judged (no budget).
* Likely cause: scalar values: mean signed error +40 %; classes: 10 of 18 match. The model's coolant effect is the friction factor; the jet's cooling and chip-breaking actions and its direction (beyond reach) do not change the predicted quantity.

### coolant-sultana2023-ti64-endmill-hpc-vs-dry

* Source: `sultana-2023-ti64-hpc-milling`, Sect. 2 and Table 4 (setup, nozzle data), Sect. 2 text (thermocouple calibration), Table 5 (L27 full factorial: T per run) (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/coolant_reach/coolant-sultana2023-ti64-endmill-hpc-vs-dry.yaml`; operation peripheral_milling; material `ti6al4v`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.coolant.lubrication.friction -> cnctwin.mechanics.coefficients (contact mean temperature).
* Settings: coating = none, edge_radius_um = 20, engagement_deg = 90, fluid = straight-oil, helix_deg = 30, tool_material = hss-m42.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.conditions.spindle_rpm` = [424, 637, 849] (derived n = 1000 vc / (pi x 12 mm); the CFD model used 850 rpm for 32 m/min).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = hss-m42 (powder-metallurgical cobalt HSS: the HSS-Co (M42) entry, db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); cutting-edge radius = 20 um (not stated in the case: the tooling model's default for this tool (hone_edge_radius_m), db/models/tooling.yaml); tool body temperature = ambient (the reading is the engagement's contact temperature; the cut's duration is not stated, est); chip thickness = mean over the engagement, fz (1 - cos phi_e) / phi_e (a thermocouple average over the cut, est); coolant fluid straight-oil = database entry (the case's fluid is not in the database: the closest database fluid, full reach of the jet, db/coolants).
* Database values used: tool material hss-m42 thermal properties (`bohler-2026-s500`); ti6al4v flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`ducobu-2017-jc-ti6al4v`); ti6al4v physical.density_kg_m3 (`aubert-duval-2018-ta6v`); ti6al4v physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v physical.conductivity_W_mK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`wojciechowski-2022-min-chip-thickness`); ti6al4v friction mu_sliding (hss, uncoated) (`est`).
* Material: database entry ti6al4v; the case says: Ti-6Al-4V grade 5, bimodal alpha+beta, 37 HRC; convex-shaped block 100 x 50 x 25 mm
* The rotary applicator (rhpc) and the single jet (hpc) differ in the model only through reach, which is 1 for both: they get the same prediction.
* Result: 27 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 31.6 %, max 149.8 %; case not judged (no budget).
* Likely cause: scalar values: mean signed error +45 %. The model's coolant effect is the friction factor; the jet's cooling and chip-breaking actions and its direction (beyond reach) do not change the predicted quantity.

## cycle_time

* Budget: none in validation/budgets.yaml.
* Verdict: not judged (no budget) - validation/budgets.yaml defines no budget for this quantity.
* Harness: `cnctwin.validation.harnesses.cycletime.cycle_time`.
* The case's toolpath (ISO dialect) through the controller executor and the motion planner and feed drives (MotionRunner, no cutting) on a three-axis machine with the stated interpolation period, acceleration, jerk, feed and contour tolerance; time from the first to the last path block.

| group | cases | cases within budget | counted values (no prediction) | median abs. error (all / values / curve points) | p90 abs. error | class matches | values within uncertainty | largest miss (tolerances) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| independent | 2 | - | 16 (0) | 7.0 % / 7.0 % / - | 21.9 % | - | 4/16 | - |

**Failures and likely causes.** No budget is defined for cycle_time. The program is the case's path in ISO syntax; the differences between the controls are carried only by their stated interpolation period, acceleration and jerk limits.

### cycletime-wilkinson2025-trochoid-840d-tnc640

* Source: `wilkinson-2025-fir-trochoidal`, Case study #2: text, Fig. 11b (plan view of the path, digitised), Table 3 (interpolator limits), Table 4 (cycle times), Listings 2-3 (NC headers) (measured, digitised, stated uncertainty 2 %).
* File: `validation/cases/cycle_time/cycletime-wilkinson2025-trochoid-840d-tnc640.yaml`; operation machine_motion; material `None`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.controller.Controller (FANUC dialect) -> cnctwin.motion.MotionDomain planner + drives -> cnctwin.motion.driver.MotionRunner.
* Settings: controller_db = fanuc-31i-b, template_machine = dmg-dmu50-3rd-siemens, tick_s = 1e-06.
* Inputs the harness assumed: G01 polygon of the 840D path = chordal deviation 5 um (segment length not stated (CAM output), est); drive data (gains, masses, rapid, max feed) = dmg-dmu50-3rd-siemens (not stated: the database machine of the same control family, db/machines); motor force limit = 10 x moving mass x the stated acceleration (the measured machine follows its control's limits; the template's own motor forces saturate at the case's jerk (excess following error). The cycle time does not change once the force is not binding (4x and 10x give the same time), est); corner mode = continuous path (blend within the tolerance) (the programs run in G64 / CYCLE832 and CYCL DEF 32 HSC mode (case settings); the template control's exact-stop default is not used, case); look-ahead depth and block processing time = 500 blocks, 1.0 ms (not stated: dmg-dmu50-3rd-siemens controller entries (est), db/machines).
* Program written in the ISO (FANUC) dialect from the case's path description; the Siemens/Heidenhain syntax of the sources is not implemented by cnctwin.controller.
* Result: 8 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 7.0 %, max 17.0 %; 2/8 within uncertainty; case not judged (no budget).
* Likely cause: errors -17 to +2 %. The planner is a time-optimal jerk-limited profile with tolerance-band corner blends; the controls' own smoothing filters (CYCLE832/COMPCAD, cycle 32 HSC) slow the feed on the circle transitions and short segments more than the bounds alone (motion.md 9).

## shear_angle

* Budget: 20 % (relative error of the simulated (mean) shear angle).
* Verdict: **fail** - only 0 of 2 cases within budget (0% < 80%); shear-orth-ti64-slusarczyk2025 misses by 2.35 tolerances (> 2).
* Harness: `cnctwin.validation.harnesses.orthogonal.shear_angle`.
* Oxley's orthogonal theory per test; the simulated shear angle is computed from the simulated chip ratio of the same kind the source used (h / t2 or chip flow speed / vc) with tan phi = r cos a / (1 - r sin a).

| group | cases | cases within budget | counted values (no prediction) | median abs. error (all / values / curve points) | p90 abs. error | class matches | values within uncertainty | largest miss (tolerances) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| independent | 2 | 0/2 | 33 (0) | 21.7 % / 21.7 % / - | 45.4 % | - | - | 2.35 (shear-orth-ti64-slusarczyk2025) |

**Failures and likely causes.** 0 of 2 independent cases within budget. Values within tolerance per case: shear-orth-ss304-marcicano2005-b 11/13; shear-orth-ti64-slusarczyk2025 5/20. Mean signed error +18 % over the independent values. The shear angle inherits the chip-ratio behaviour (budgets.yaml: d phi / phi = 0.8-0.95 x the chip-ratio error): almost constant in the model (set by the database friction, Ti6Al4V mu = 0.3 from the ETH Albrecht analysis, 304 mu = 0.6), rising with the uncut thickness in the measurements.

### shear-orth-ss304-marcicano2005-b

* Source: `marcicano-2005-aisi304-cutting-tests`, Table 4 (maker B), column phi_n (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/shear_angle/shear-orth-ss304-marcicano2005-b.yaml`; operation orthogonal_turning; material `aisi304`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.materials.WorkMaterial/ToolSide (databases) -> cnctwin.mechanics.contact.min_chip_thickness -> cnctwin.mechanics.oxley.solve (Oxley 1989 with Johnson-Cook, Coulomb-Tresca interface, rake heat partition at the tool temperature).
* Settings: coating = none, h_min_ratio = 0.27, mu_sliding = 0.6, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, tool_temperature = ambient (tool body at the start of a short test), work_temperature_K = 293.1.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.clearance_deg` = 6 (assumed: not stated: ISO 1832 letter P = 11 deg insert clearance (code convention, standard not read) minus the 5 deg positive tilt that gives the stated rake); `setup.tool.edge_radius_um` = 15 (assumed: not stated; typical of a ground (G-tolerance) uncoated insert edge (est.)); `setup.tool.nose_radius_mm` = 0.8 (assumed: ISO code '08' = 0.8 mm corner radius (code convention); outside the cut when the tube wall lies on the straight edge); `setup.conditions.coolant` = dry (assumed: not stated; dry orthogonal tests are implied by the analysis).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (grade not in the database: the carbide class default, db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); aisi304 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`zou-2026-304-min-cutting-thickness`); aisi304 physical.density_kg_m3 (`thyssenkrupp-2020-14301`); aisi304 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`thyssenkrupp-2020-14301`); aisi304 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`outokumpu-steel-grades`); aisi304 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`zou-2026-304-min-cutting-thickness`); aisi304 friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`zou-2026-304-min-cutting-thickness`).
* Material: database entry aisi304; the case says: annealed AISI 304 tubes; quasi-static compression gives JC A 360 MPa, B 1244 MPa, n 0.72 (Table 1); hardness not stated
* Shear angle compared as the source computed it: from the chip thickness ratio h / h_c.
* Result: 13 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 10.8 %, max 46.3 %; case **outside budget**; largest miss 2.31 tolerances.
* Likely cause: simulated shear angle (deg) 24.1-25.5 against measured 17.4-29.5 (mean signed error +5 %); database sliding friction mu = 0.6, h_min ratio 0.27. With the Coulomb-Tresca interface in the sliding regime, Oxley's shear angle is set mainly by the friction angle (lambda = atan mu) and the strain-hardening term, so the simulated ratio barely changes with speed and feed while the measured one varies.

### shear-orth-ti64-slusarczyk2025

* Source: `slusarczyk-2025-ti6al4v-rake-temperature`, Table 4 (shear angle phi), Table 5 (average chip flow speed from high-speed video, 3000 fps) (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/shear_angle/shear-orth-ti64-slusarczyk2025.yaml`; operation orthogonal_turning; material `ti6al4v`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.materials.WorkMaterial/ToolSide (databases) -> cnctwin.mechanics.contact.min_chip_thickness -> cnctwin.mechanics.oxley.solve (Oxley 1989 with Johnson-Cook, Coulomb-Tresca interface, rake heat partition at the tool temperature).
* Settings: coating = none, h_min_ratio = 0.37, mu_sliding = 0.3, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, tool_temperature = ambient (tool body at the start of a short test), work_temperature_K = 293.1.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.edge_radius_um` = 10 (assumed: not stated; typical of a ground uncoated WC-Co edge (est.)); `setup.conditions.coolant` = dry (assumed: not stated; the side-view thermography of the cutting zone implies no coolant).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (uncoated straight WC-Co: the straight WC-6Co K20 entry, db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); ti6al4v flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`ducobu-2017-jc-ti6al4v`); ti6al4v physical.density_kg_m3 (`aubert-duval-2018-ta6v`); ti6al4v physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v physical.conductivity_W_mK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`wojciechowski-2022-min-chip-thickness`); ti6al4v friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`klippel-2024-ck45-orthogonal`).
* Material: database entry ti6al4v; the case says: Ti-6Al-4V shaft; only the composition is given (Table 1: Al 5.5-6.75, V 3.5-4.5, Fe 0.03, O 0.20, C 0.08, N 0.05, H 0.015 wt%); condition not stated
* Shear angle compared as the source computed it: from the chip flow speed ratio V_chip / vc.
* Result: 20 compared, 0 without prediction, 20 not compared; median abs. error 24.3 %, max 47.1 %; case **outside budget**; largest miss 2.35 tolerances.
* Likely cause: simulated shear angle (deg) 29.6-37.4 against measured 23.6-30.3 (mean signed error +26 %); database sliding friction mu = 0.3, h_min ratio 0.37. With the Coulomb-Tresca interface in the sliding regime, Oxley's shear angle is set mainly by the friction angle (lambda = atan mu) and the strain-hardening term, so the simulated ratio barely changes with speed and feed while the measured one varies.

## spindle_growth

* Budget: none in validation/budgets.yaml.
* Verdict: not judged (no budget) - validation/budgets.yaml defines no budget for this quantity.
* Harness: `cnctwin.validation.harnesses.spindle.spindle_growth`.
* Thermal's spindle body (first-order, Palmgren bearing heat) at the case's constant speeds from a cold start; the machine's spindle data are not published, so the model's own defaults and the database VMC's maximum speed are used (listed).

| group | cases | cases within budget | counted values (no prediction) | median abs. error (all / values / curve points) | p90 abs. error | class matches | values within uncertainty | largest miss (tolerances) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| independent | 4 | - | 320 (0) | 92.8 % / 93.5 % / 92.8 % | 95.5 % | - | 5/320 | - |

**Failures and likely causes.** No budget is defined for spindle_growth. None of the papers describes the spindle (drive, bearings, cooling, maximum speed), so every spindle parameter is a model default: the result measures how far the defaults are from these two machines, not the model's physics. A case with a named spindle and its data would test the model (GAP.md).

### spindlegrowth-wei2023-vcenter55-ambient-cold

* Source: `wei-2023-vcenter55-thermal-error`, Sect. 2.1-2.3, Tables 1-3 (Table 2: K1 3.7 degC initial ambient, 2.2 K rise; K3 6.2 degC, 3.8 K rise), Fig. 4 Z panel (curves K7, K1, K3, digitised) (measured, digitised, stated uncertainty 3 %).
* File: `validation/cases/spindle_growth/spindlegrowth-wei2023-vcenter55-ambient-cold.yaml`; operation spindle_warmup; material `None`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.thermal.machine.spindle_models (first-order spindle body).
* Settings: bearing_heat_at_max_W = 400, growth_at_max_speed_um = 20, growth_length_m = 0.25, max_rpm = 1.2e+04, speed_exponent = 1.667, step_s = 60, time_constant_min = 25.
* Inputs the harness assumed: ambient of Z_thermal_error_K7_2000rpm = 8.4 degC (initial ambient not given: middle of the case's ambient band, case); maximum spindle speed = 12000 rpm (not stated: the database vertical machining centre haas-vf2ss, db/machines); spindle growth = 2e-05 (SI) (not stated (no spindle data in the source): the thermal model's default, db/models/thermal-machine.yaml); spindle time constant = 1500 (SI) (not stated (no spindle data in the source): the thermal model's default, db/models/thermal-machine.yaml); spindle bearing heat = 400 (SI) (not stated (no spindle data in the source): the thermal model's default, db/models/thermal-machine.yaml); thermal compensation = off (ISO 230-3 measures the uncompensated error, case).
* The measured Z error is the whole tool-to-table loop (spindle, headstock, column); the model is the spindle body only.
* Result: 68 compared, 0 without prediction, 3 not compared; median abs. error 93.0 %, max 97.1 %; 0/68 within uncertainty; case not judged (no budget).
* Likely cause: mean signed error -92 %: generic spindle defaults (20 um at the assumed 12 000 rpm, 25 min) against a measured whole-machine loop; the heat scales as (n/n_max)^(5/3), so at 2000-6000 rpm the model's growth is a small fraction of its default maximum.

### spindlegrowth-yang2024-vmc850-constant-speed

* Source: `yang-2024-vmc850-thermal-error`, Sect. 3.1-3.2 (machine, sensors, test conditions), Sect. 4.1 (temperature and deformation behaviour), Fig. 11a,b (Z-axis thermal deformation, digitised) (measured, digitised, stated uncertainty 5 %).
* File: `validation/cases/spindle_growth/spindlegrowth-yang2024-vmc850-constant-speed.yaml`; operation spindle_warmup; material `None`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.thermal.machine.spindle_models (first-order spindle body).
* Settings: bearing_heat_at_max_W = 400, growth_at_max_speed_um = 20, growth_length_m = 0.25, max_rpm = 1.2e+04, speed_exponent = 1.667, step_s = 60, time_constant_min = 25.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.conditions.machine_state_at_start` = cold start (assumed: the paper says the machine temperature is low after switching on; the time since the previous run is not stated).
* Inputs the harness assumed: ambient of Z_thermal_deformation_2000rpm = 20 degC (not stated: ISO 1 reference temperature, est); maximum spindle speed = 12000 rpm (not stated: the database vertical machining centre haas-vf2ss, db/machines); spindle growth = 2e-05 (SI) (not stated (no spindle data in the source): the thermal model's default, db/models/thermal-machine.yaml); spindle time constant = 1500 (SI) (not stated (no spindle data in the source): the thermal model's default, db/models/thermal-machine.yaml); spindle bearing heat = 400 (SI) (not stated (no spindle data in the source): the thermal model's default, db/models/thermal-machine.yaml); thermal compensation = off (ISO 230-3 measures the uncompensated error, case); ambient of Z_thermal_deformation_4000rpm = 20 degC (not stated: ISO 1 reference temperature, est).
* The measured Z error is the whole tool-to-table loop (spindle, headstock, column); the model is the spindle body only.
* Result: 112 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 94.4 %, max 95.5 %; 5/112 within uncertainty; case not judged (no budget).
* Likely cause: mean signed error -87 %: generic spindle defaults (20 um at the assumed 12 000 rpm, 25 min) against a measured whole-machine loop; the heat scales as (n/n_max)^(5/3), so at 2000-6000 rpm the model's growth is a small fraction of its default maximum.

## taylor_life

* Budget: none in validation/budgets.yaml.
* Verdict: not judged (no budget) - validation/budgets.yaml defines no budget for this quantity.
* Harness: `cnctwin.validation.harnesses.taylor.taylor_life`.
* The wear law as the wear domain builds it (published Usui constants, else calibrated to the database Taylor entry with the mechanics response), integrated from a fresh edge to the case's VB (or VN) criterion at each tested speed, feed and depth (equivalent orthogonal cut, h = f).

| group | cases | cases within budget | counted values (no prediction) | median abs. error (all / values / curve points) | p90 abs. error | class matches | values within uncertainty | largest miss (tolerances) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| independent | 5 | - | 94 (2) | 99.5 % / 100.0 % / 99.3 % | 214.8 % | - | 0/94 | - |
| calibration-dependent | 2 | - | 21 (0) | 13.9 % / 13.9 % / - | 30.4 % | - | 5/21 | - |

**Failures and likely causes.** No budget is defined for taylor_life. Where the wear law is calibrated to a database Taylor entry, the prediction can be no better than that entry for the case's tool and material (most entries are estimates); where the same paper supplied the entry the case is calibration-dependent and shows only that the integration reproduces its own fit. Ceramic, cermet and PCBN pairings rest on estimated Taylor entries or published Usui sets from other tests.

### taylor-xue2022-wg300-in718-dry

* Source: `xue-2022-in718-ceramic-tool-wear`, Sect. 2 and Table 3 (setup, criteria), Sect. 3.2 (inspection every 30 s, failure modes), Fig. 2a-b (VB and VN versus cutting time, digitised) (measured, digitised, stated uncertainty 2 %).
* File: `validation/cases/taylor_life/taylor-xue2022-wg300-in718-dry.yaml`; operation longitudinal_turning; material `inconel718`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.wear.build.wear_law -> cnctwin.wear.calibration.MechanicsResponse (cnctwin.mechanics Oxley + Smithey land + Blok partition) -> life integral with cnctwin.wear.kernels.usui_rate.
* Settings: A_per_Pa = [4.36482e-13], B_K = [20645.8], VB_per_VN = 9.619, calibration_residuals = [-1.28786e-14, -2.22045e-15, 0.0881946], clearance_deg = 6, coating = none, edge_radius_um = 8, flank_law = taylor, notch_factor = 2.19, rake_deg = -6, taylor_entry = inconel718 tool_life[1] (ceramic, uncoated), taylor_relation = substrate, taylor_src = est, tool_material = ceramic-whisker.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.edge_preparation` = T-land 0.10 mm x 20 deg (decoded from the edge code T01020 of the designation; not stated in words in the source); `setup.tool.inscribed_circle_mm` = 12.7 (decoded from the ISO designation (size 12 of a square insert = 1/2 in IC); not stated in the source); `setup.tool.nose_radius_mm` = 1.6 (decoded from the ISO designation (nose-radius code 16 = 1.6 mm); the coding standard was not read in this run).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = ceramic-whisker (SiC-whisker reinforced alumina, db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); cutting-edge radius = 8 um (not stated in the case: the tooling model's default for this tool (chamfer_corner_radius_m), db/models/tooling.yaml).
* Database values used: inconel718 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`qiu-2019-in718-jc-sensitivity`); inconel718 physical.density_kg_m3 (`special-metals-2007-in718`); inconel718 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`wang-2018-in718-chip-formation`); inconel718 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`special-metals-2007-in718`); inconel718 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`est`); inconel718 friction mu_sliding (ceramic, uncoated) (`est`); inconel718 tool_life[1] (ceramic, uncoated) (`est`).
* Material: database entry inconel718; the case says: solution-treated and aged IN718 bar (source Table 2: Rm 1430 MPa, Rp0.2 1300 MPa, 40 HRC, 14.7 W/m K; Table 1: Ni 52.15, Cr 19.26, Nb 5.03, Mo 3.03, Ti 1.08, Al 0.56 wt%, Fe bal.)
* Wear model: tool slot 0: grain size 1 um assumed for BUE attrition.
* Result: 37 compared, 0 without prediction, 8 not compared; median abs. error 98.9 %, max 1043.6 %; 0/37 within uncertainty; case not judged (no budget).
* Likely cause: mean signed error of the lives +413 %; flank law: taylor (calibrated to inconel718 tool_life[1] (ceramic, uncoated), src est); VB(t) curves: mean signed error -99 % (the model's land grows slowly while it is small: the flank temperature rises with the land).

* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.nose_radius_mm` = 0.8 (decoded from the designation (code 08); not stated in words).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (Kennametal K313 is an uncoated straight WC-Co K grade, db/tool_materials); coating = none (not stated in the case: uncoated assumed, case); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); coolant fluid emulsion-5pct = friction factor 0.89 (the case's fluid is not in the database: the database emulsion-5pct entry, full reach, db/coolants); coolant fluid mql-ester = friction factor 0.84 (the case's fluid is not in the database: the database mql-ester entry, full reach, db/coolants).

* Material: database entry ti6al4v; the case says: Ti-6Al-4V heat treated 1-4 h at 700-800 C and air cooled, 91.7 % alpha / 8.3 % beta (Sect. 2.1; Tables 1-3 of the source give composition and properties)
* Result: 14 compared, 2 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 100.0 %, max 100.0 %; 0/16 within uncertainty; case not judged (no budget).

## tool_temperature

* Budget: 15 % (relative error of the simulated temperature rise above ambient at the measured location (IR window average, or contact-area average for a tool-work thermocouple)).
* Verdict: **fail** - only 1 of 5 cases within budget (20% < 80%); tooltemp-turn-aisi4140-kus2015-ir-tc misses by 12.45 tolerances (> 2).
* Harness: `cnctwin.validation.harnesses.temperature.tool_temperature`.
* The contact through the coefficient pipeline (cnctwin.mechanics.coefficients: Oxley rows, edge forces, partition at the tool body temperature) coupled to thermal's tool ladder of the insert; readings as measured: natural thermocouple = mean interface temperature, IR window or spot and embedded thermocouple = the tool temperature at the location (body + local edge field, thermal's edge-model composition).

| group | cases | cases within budget | counted values (no prediction) | median abs. error (all / values / curve points) | p90 abs. error | class matches | values within uncertainty | largest miss (tolerances) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| independent | 5 | 1/5 | 108 (0) | 52.8 % / 45.1 % / 60.7 % | 86.8 % | - | 1/108 | 12.4 (tooltemp-turn-aisi4140-kus2015-ir-tc) |

**Failures and likely causes.** 1 of 5 independent cases within budget (tooltemp-orth-ti64-loschner2025-ntc). Values within tolerance per case: tooltemp-orth-ti64-loschner2025-ntc 17/17; tooltemp-orth-ti64-slusarczyk2025-ir 0/20; tooltemp-thinwall-al7050-zawada2025-ntc 6/9; tooltemp-turn-aisi1045-guimaraes2023-embedded-tc 4/6; tooltemp-turn-aisi4140-kus2015-ir-tc 0/56. Temperatures are compared as rises above ambient. Readings at a distance from the contact (IR window and spot) come out low because thermal's tool model resolves the contact flash and a 1-D insert body, not the 3-D field in the first millimetres of the insert; readings inside the insert depend on the heat into the insert, the seat and holder contact and the cutting time. No coefficient was adjusted; the cutting times and sensor positions the sources leave open are listed per case.

### tooltemp-orth-ti64-loschner2025-ntc

* Source: `loschner-2025-ti6al4v-jc-sensitivity`, Fig. 2c, experiment lines (digitised; 3.77 C per px) (measured, digitised, stated uncertainty 2 %).

* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.coefficients.build_process_map/group_coefficients -> thermal partition (rake/edge shares, thermal/partition.py convention) -> cnctwin.thermal.toolmodel.ToolThermal (insert ladder) -> cnctwin.thermal.kernels.moving_rect_rise (local edge field).
* Settings: clearance_deg = 7, coating = none, edge_radius_um = 15, entering_angle_deg = 90, ladder_step_s = 0.25, mu_sliding = 0.3, rake_deg = 20, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, wedge_factor = 1.429.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.edge_radius_um` = 15 (assumed: not stated; typical honed edge of a ground carbide grooving insert (est.)).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (grade not in the database: the carbide class default, db/tool_materials); coating = none (not stated in the case: uncoated assumed, case); cutting time = 5 s (duration of a turning test not stated: 5 s of stable cutting assumed, est); insert inscribed circle = 12.70 mm (not stated: thermal model default (default_insert_ic_m), db/models/thermal-tool.yaml); insert thickness = 4.76 mm (not stated: thermal model default (default_insert_thickness_m), db/models/thermal-tool.yaml); holder bar section = 25 x 25 mm (not stated: thermal model default (default_bar_section_m), db/models/thermal-tool.yaml); holder bar overhang = 30 mm (not stated: thermal model default (default_bar_overhang_m), db/models/thermal-tool.yaml).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); ti6al4v flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`ducobu-2017-jc-ti6al4v`); ti6al4v physical.density_kg_m3 (`aubert-duval-2018-ta6v`); ti6al4v physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v physical.conductivity_W_mK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`wojciechowski-2022-min-chip-thickness`); ti6al4v friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`klippel-2024-ck45-orthogonal`).
* Material: database entry ti6al4v; the case says: Ti6Al4V; heat treatment, hardness and supplier data are not given in the source (DB entry: mill annealed)
* Sensor: natural (tool-work) thermocouple: contact-area mean interface temperature (case notes).
* Chip section of the turning cases: h = f sin(kappa_r), width = ap / sin(kappa_r) (straight edge; the nose radius is not resolved).
* Result: 17 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 6.6 %, max 11.5 %; 1/17 within uncertainty; case within budget; largest miss 0.765 tolerances.

* Source: `slusarczyk-2025-ti6al4v-rake-temperature`, Table 5 (average contact temperature from thermograms in the stable cutting phase), Sect. 3.2 and 4 (measured, stated uncertainty 8 %).
* File: `validation/cases/tool_temperature/tooltemp-orth-ti64-slusarczyk2025-ir.yaml`; operation orthogonal_turning; material `ti6al4v`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.coefficients.build_process_map/group_coefficients -> thermal partition (rake/edge shares, thermal/partition.py convention) -> cnctwin.thermal.toolmodel.ToolThermal (insert ladder) -> cnctwin.thermal.kernels.moving_rect_rise (local edge field).
* Settings: clearance_deg = 5, coating = none, edge_radius_um = 10, entering_angle_deg = 90, ladder_step_s = 0.25, mu_sliding = 0.3, rake_deg = 0, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, wedge_factor = 1.059.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.edge_radius_um` = 10 (assumed: not stated; typical of a ground uncoated WC-Co edge (est.)); `setup.conditions.coolant` = dry (assumed: not stated; the side-view thermography of the cutting zone implies no coolant).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (uncoated straight WC-Co: the straight WC-6Co K20 entry, db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); cutting time = 5 s (duration of a turning test not stated: 5 s of stable cutting assumed, est); insert inscribed circle = 12.70 mm (not stated: thermal model default (default_insert_ic_m), db/models/thermal-tool.yaml); insert thickness = 4.76 mm (not stated: thermal model default (default_insert_thickness_m), db/models/thermal-tool.yaml); holder bar section = 25 x 25 mm (not stated: thermal model default (default_bar_section_m), db/models/thermal-tool.yaml); holder bar overhang = 30 mm (not stated: thermal model default (default_bar_overhang_m), db/models/thermal-tool.yaml).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); ti6al4v flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`ducobu-2017-jc-ti6al4v`); ti6al4v physical.density_kg_m3 (`aubert-duval-2018-ta6v`); ti6al4v physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v physical.conductivity_W_mK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`wojciechowski-2022-min-chip-thickness`); ti6al4v friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`klippel-2024-ck45-orthogonal`).
* Material: database entry ti6al4v; the case says: Ti-6Al-4V shaft; only the composition is given (Table 1: Al 5.5-6.75, V 3.5-4.5, Fe 0.03, O 0.20, C 0.08, N 0.05, H 0.015 wt%); condition not stated
* Sensor: 'mean apparent temperature of a 2 x 1 mm area on the tool, seen from the side ... next to the chip-tool contact' (case notes).
* Chip section of the turning cases: h = f sin(kappa_r), width = ap / sin(kappa_r) (straight edge; the nose radius is not resolved).
* Result: 20 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 45.1 %, max 65.9 %; 0/20 within uncertainty; case **outside budget**; largest miss 3.93 tolerances.
* Likely cause: side_window: mean signed error of the rise -47 % (beyond about 2 max(l_c, l_f, edge element) from the edge thermal's composition leaves only the 1-D insert body (a cross-section mean of the whole insert), so a window 0-2 mm from the edge is read nearly at the body temperature, while the measured window sees the steep 3-D field near the contact (and the source's assumed emissivity 0.48)).

### tooltemp-thinwall-al7050-zawada2025-ntc

* Source: `zawada-2025-al7050-thin-wall-forces`, Sect. 2 (natural thermocouple circuit, graphite brush, HBM 1-MX840A at 10 kHz, calibration stand and Eq. 1), Sect. 3.2 (definition of T), Table A1 (T for the 13 runs) (measured, stated uncertainty 1 %).
* File: `validation/cases/tool_temperature/tooltemp-thinwall-al7050-zawada2025-ntc.yaml`; operation peripheral_milling; material `al7075-t7351`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.coefficients.build_process_map/group_coefficients -> thermal partition (rake/edge shares, thermal/partition.py convention).
* Settings: clearance_deg = 6, coating = none, edge_radius_um = 20, entering_angle_deg = 90, ladder_step_s = 0.25, mu_sliding = 0.55, rake_deg = 15, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, wedge_factor = 1.304.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.rake_deg` = 15 (assumed: not stated (typical of aluminium end mills)); `setup.conditions.ambient_temp_degC` = 20 (assumed: not stated).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (grade not in the database: the carbide class default, db/tool_materials); coating = none (case coating 'TiB2' has no database entry: modelled uncoated, db/coatings); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); clearance angle = 6 deg (not stated in the case, est); cutting-edge radius = 20 um (not stated in the case: the tooling model's default for this tool (hone_edge_radius_m), db/models/tooling.yaml); tool body temperature (milling) = ambient (one short pass per test; the engagement's transient contact (contact time = engagement angle / omega) is in the partition, est).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); al7075-t7351 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`est`); al7075-t7351 physical.density_kg_m3 (`kaiser-2006-al7075-datasheet`); al7075-t7351 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`muraca-1972-al7075-handbook`); al7075-t7351 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`kaiser-2006-al7075-datasheet`); al7075-t7351 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`wojciechowski-2022-min-chip-thickness`); al7075-t7351 friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`est`).
* Material: database entry al7075-t7351; the case says: 7050-T7451 plate (AMS 4050K), not in the DB; see force-thinwall-al7050-zawada2025-finish for details
* Sensor: natural thermocouple, mean of ten peak values: peak contact temperature of the engagement (case notes).
* Result: 9 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 12.6 %, max 53.7 %; 0/9 within uncertainty; case **outside budget**; largest miss 3.58 tolerances.
* Likely cause: interface_peak_milling: mean signed error of the rise +25 % (a 3-6 um aluminium chip at a 20 um hone (tooling default): the flank contact of the edge dominates the contact mean; minimum chip thickness and edge radius are estimates).

### tooltemp-turn-aisi1045-guimaraes2023-embedded-tc

* Source: `guimaraes-2023-aisi1045-embedded-thermocouple`, Sect. 2.1-2.4 (insert, thermocouple position, calibration, lathe, Table 2 conditions, IR camera), Sect. 3 text and Conclusions (maximum temperatures and times), Figs. 5-6 (temperature versus machining time, not digitised) (measured, stated uncertainty 1 %).
* File: `validation/cases/tool_temperature/tooltemp-turn-aisi1045-guimaraes2023-embedded-tc.yaml`; operation longitudinal_turning; material `aisi1045`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.coefficients.build_process_map/group_coefficients -> thermal partition (rake/edge shares, thermal/partition.py convention) -> cnctwin.thermal.toolmodel.ToolThermal (insert ladder) -> cnctwin.thermal.kernels.moving_rect_rise (local edge field).
* Settings: clearance_deg = 6, coating = none, edge_radius_um = 35, entering_angle_deg = 95, ladder_step_s = 0.25, mu_sliding = 0.5, rake_deg = -6, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, wedge_factor = 1.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.clearance_deg` = 6 (assumed with the holder above); `setup.tool.entering_angle_deg` = 95 (assumed with the holder above); `setup.tool.holder` = PCLNR 2020K12-type lever holder (assumed: the holder is not named; a standard negative CNMG holder gives rake -6, clearance 6, inclination -6, entering angle 95 deg (judgement, not read)); `setup.tool.nose_radius_mm` = 0.8 (decoded from the designation (code 08); not stated in words); `setup.tool.rake_deg` = -6 (assumed with the holder above); `setup.conditions.ambient_temp_degC` = 25 (assumed: not stated (the calibration started at 25 C)).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (grade not in the database: the carbide class default, db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 25 degC (as the case assumes, case); cutting-edge radius = 35 um (not stated in the case: the tooling model's default for this tool (insert_edge_radius_m), db/models/tooling.yaml); cutting time at vc 40 m/min = 150.9 s (pass length / (f n) with the bar diameter 52.5 mm implied by the 70 m/min test (its maximum at 86.2 s taken as its pass end), case); insert inscribed circle = 12.70 mm (not stated: thermal model default (default_insert_ic_m), db/models/thermal-tool.yaml); insert thickness = 4.76 mm (not stated: thermal model default (default_insert_thickness_m), db/models/thermal-tool.yaml); holder bar section = 25 x 25 mm (not stated: thermal model default (default_bar_section_m), db/models/thermal-tool.yaml); holder bar overhang = 30 mm (not stated: thermal model default (default_bar_overhang_m), db/models/thermal-tool.yaml).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); aisi1045 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`burns-2012-1045-cutting-temperature`); aisi1045 physical.density_kg_m3 (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`est`); aisi1045 friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`est`).
* Material: database entry aisi1045; the case says: AISI 1045; condition, hardness and bar diameter are not given in the source
* Sensor: K-type junction in the rake face 2.9 mm from the cutting edge, 0.2-0.4 mm deep (setup.tool.thermocouple, case notes).
* Chip section of the turning cases: h = f sin(kappa_r), width = ap / sin(kappa_r) (straight edge; the nose radius is not resolved).
* Result: 6 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 4.9 %, max 31.5 %; 0/6 within uncertainty; case **outside budget**; largest miss 2.1 tolerances.
* Likely cause: point: mean signed error of the rise -4 % (the junction sits in the insert body: the 1-D ladder heats the insert as a whole (seat contact, holder bar and cutting time are estimates or derived)).

### tooltemp-turn-aisi4140-kus2015-ir-tc

* Source: `kus-2015-4140-thermocouple-ir-turning`, Table 1 (setup), sections 'Infrared Pyrometer', 'The Thermocouple' and 'Experimental Procedure' (sensor positions, averaging, repeats), Table 5 (results), Fig. 7 (thermocouple position), Fig. 10 (thermocouple readings versus cutting time, digitised) (measured, digitised, stated uncertainty 1 %).
* File: `validation/cases/tool_temperature/tooltemp-turn-aisi4140-kus2015-ir-tc.yaml`; operation longitudinal_turning; material `aisi4140-qt30`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.coefficients.build_process_map/group_coefficients -> thermal partition (rake/edge shares, thermal/partition.py convention) -> cnctwin.thermal.toolmodel.ToolThermal (insert ladder) -> cnctwin.thermal.kernels.moving_rect_rise (local edge field).
* Settings: clearance_deg = 6, coating = tialn, edge_radius_um = 35, entering_angle_deg = 95, ladder_step_s = 0.25, mu_sliding = 0.4, rake_deg = -6, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, wedge_factor = 1.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.entering_angle_deg` = 95 (assumed: not stated; the L in the DWLNR holder code denotes a 95 deg approach (ISO holder coding, not read in this run)); `setup.conditions.ambient_temp_degC` = 25 (assumed: not stated for the tests; the source's FE model used ambient 20-30 C); `setup.conditions.cut_duration_s` = 20 (read from Fig. 10, whose thermocouple readings run from 5 to 20 s of cutting; the duration of the Table 5 tests is not stated).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (grade not in the database: the carbide class default, db/tool_materials); coating = tialn (case coating 'PVD TiAlN-TiN' mapped to the database coating tialn, db/coatings); ambient temperature = 25 degC (as the case assumes, case); cutting-edge radius = 35 um (not stated in the case: the tooling model's default for this tool (insert_edge_radius_m), db/models/tooling.yaml); insert inscribed circle = 12.70 mm (not stated: thermal model default (default_insert_ic_m), db/models/thermal-tool.yaml); holder bar overhang = 30 mm (not stated: thermal model default (default_bar_overhang_m), db/models/thermal-tool.yaml).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); aisi4140-qt30 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`johnson-cook-1983`); aisi4140-qt30 physical.density_kg_m3 (`thyssenkrupp-2017-42crmo4`); aisi4140-qt30 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`est`); aisi4140-qt30 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`est`); aisi4140-qt30 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`est`); aisi4140-qt30 friction mu_sliding (carbide, TiAlN) (`est`).
* Material: database entry aisi4140-qt30; the case says: AISI 4140 heat treated to 50 HRC (induction hardened, then described as solution treated, oil quenched and normalised; the conclusions state 'heat-treated AISI 4140, hardness 50 HRC'). The DB entry is Q&T 30 HRC, so the flow stress must be raised to the 50 HRC condition. Table 2 composition: C 0.38, Cr 0.80, Mn 0.75, Si 0.15, Mo 0.15, P 0.035, S 0.04 and 'Ni 9.58' (an obvious misprint for 4140)
* Sensor: pyrometer spot 1.5 mm on the rake face 1 mm from the edge beside the chip, readings averaged over the cut (setup.conditions.ir_spot).
* Sensor: K-type, 1 mm from the flank face and 1 mm below the rake face at the corner (setup.conditions.thermocouple); readings at the cutting times of Fig. 10.
* Sensor: as the transient curves; Table 5 values are the mean of the readings over 5-20 s (case notes).
* Chip section of the turning cases: h = f sin(kappa_r), width = ap / sin(kappa_r) (straight edge; the nose radius is not resolved).
* Result: 56 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 76.8 %, max 186.7 %; 0/56 within uncertainty; case **outside budget**; largest miss 12.4 tolerances.
* Likely cause: point: mean signed error of the rise +75 % (the junction sits in the insert body: the 1-D ladder heats the insert as a whole (seat contact, holder bar and cutting time are estimates or derived)); rake_spot: mean signed error of the rise -81 % (the same limitation 1 mm from the edge (body + local field, the local field cut at r_far); the material is the database's 30 HRC entry for a 50 HRC bar).
